  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    const shouldBeDark = saved === "dark";
    setIsDark(shouldBeDark);

  new_string: // Theme Toggle Button for TopNav
function ThemeToggleButton() {
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    return saved === "dark";
  });
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    const shouldBeDark = saved === "dark";
    if (shouldBeDark !== isDark) {
      setIsDark(shouldBeDark);
    }
    
    if (shouldBeDark) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark");
    }
  }, [isDark]);

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Shield } from "lucide-react";

export default function AAAHeader() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Get user info from token or API
    const token = localStorage.getItem("auth_token");
    if (token) {
      // Decode token or fetch user info
      try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(atob(token.split(".")[1]));
        setUser({ email: payload.email || "admin@shiftwave.com" });
      } catch (e) {
        setUser({ email: "admin@shiftwave.com" });
      }
    }
  }, []);
  new_string: "use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Shield } from "lucide-react";

interface UserInfo {
  email: string;
}

export default function AAAHeader() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserInfo | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Get user info from token or API
    const token = localStorage.getItem("auth_token");
    if (token) {

  new_string: export function ThemeToggle() {
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    return saved === "dark";
  });
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    // Check localStorage - default to light mode
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    
    const shouldBeDark = saved === "dark";
    if (shouldBeDark !== isDark) {
      setIsDark(shouldBeDark);
    }
    
    if (shouldBeDark) {

    }
  }, [isDark]);

      return;
    }
    
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await crmApi.getTenantDashboard(tenantId);
      setDashboard(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error fetching dashboard:", error);
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      // If tenant not found, show error message
      if (errorMessage.includes("404") || errorMessage.includes("not found")) {
        setDashboard(null);
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [tenantId]);

        // Log error for debugging

        // If timeout, return null - page still works
        return null;
      });

      const timeLabel = `${now.getHours()}:${now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
      
      // CPU History
      if (json.load_avg?.["1min"] && typeof json.load_avg["1min"] === 'number') {
        setCpuHistory(prev => {
          const newData = { label: timeLabel, value: json.load_avg["1min"] };
          const updated = [...prev, newData];
          return updated.slice(-30); // Keep last 30 data points
        });
      }
      
      // Memory History
      if (json.memory && json.memory.total && json.memory.available !== undefined) {
        const memoryPercent = ((json.memory.total - json.memory.available) / json.memory.total) * 100;
        if (typeof memoryPercent === 'number' && !isNaN(memoryPercent)) {
          setMemoryHistory(prev => {
            const newData = { label: timeLabel, value: memoryPercent };
            const updated = [...prev, newData];
            return updated.slice(-30);
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Network History
      if (json.network?.stats?.total) {
        const rxValue = json.network.stats.total.rx_mbps || 0;
        const txValue = json.network.stats.total.tx_mbps || 0;
        if (typeof rxValue === 'number') {
          setNetworkRxHistory(prev => {
            const newData = { label: timeLabel, value: rxValue };
            const updated = [...prev, newData];
            return updated.slice(-30);
          });
        }
        if (typeof txValue === 'number') {
          setNetworkTxHistory(prev => {
            const newData = { label: timeLabel, value: txValue };
            const updated = [...prev, newData];
            return updated.slice(-30);
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Also fetch Prometheus metrics (don't wait for it)
      fetchPrometheusMetrics().catch(() => {
        // Ignore Prometheus errors - it's optional
      });
    } catch (err) {
      // Silently fail - page still works without data
      console.warn("Monitor data fetch failed (non-blocking):", err);
      
      // Only show error on first load if we have no data at all
      // Error state removed - page works without showing errors
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      fetchingRef.current = false;
    }
